**Wave-1 close (R25, 2026-11-07).** Wave 1 is closed under R25 at HEAD ab03da9. Each condition:
- **Two review rounds in a row with nothing above minor.** Review rounds 1-4 confirmed 22, 14, 16 and 13 findings. Rounds 5-10 confirmed 4, 9, 2, 5, 3 and 3, all minor, so every pair of consecutive rounds from round 5 on holds nothing above minor.
- **Perf acceptance green at HEAD.** The Goal-1 acceptance re-run at bee4236 (attempt 2, recorded in ab03da9; §9.1 and §9.4) passed every cell. ab03da9 changes docs only.
- **Full verify green at HEAD.** The full verify at ab03da9 was green: fmt, the 3 clippy configs, the workspace tests (1,046 passed), `-p horde-defense --no-default-features` (521) and `--features dev` (578), `--smoke`, check_views, both audio generators' `--check`, `run_script_tests.ps1` (31 cases), and `pacing 60 --runs` byte-identical to the post-W1-69 baseline `target/production-after/w1-69/pacing.txt`. Evidence: `target/production-after/wave1/verify-final/`.
- **Every confirmed finding fixed or ruled.** Every finding of rounds 5-10 is fixed except the Invoke-Native item, which R24 resolves. The wave-1 close rulings are R12-R25. Under R25, minor findings still open would carry into the wave-2 review; rounds 5-10 leave none open.

Wave 1 hands on these open items:
- **`pacing_targets` (W2-03).** `tests/pacing_targets.rs` does not exist yet, so the wave-1 release run (`target/production-after/wave1/pacing_targets.log`) matched 0 tests. W2-03 creates the goal-5 evaluators and the ignored goal5_* tests, and W2-04 adds the goal6_* tests.
- **The W1-69 balance shift (wave 2).** The novice Germany profile went from 5 to 8 defeats and its first victory moved from run 3 to run 5; smoke failed attempts went Personal 2 → 1 and Npc 1 → 3 (§9.6). Balance was not retuned in wave 1. Wave 2's pacing work owns the shift, measured against the post-W1-69 baseline.
- **Gate 3 (W2-50).** The sample-932 stall-tail frame from the 867f786 re-run and the W1-61 stress-on-r2 episode stay open. W2-50 owns both, before the W2-47 re-gate. Gate 3 is not claimed clean in dev VSync-on stress or in the shipped default configuration (§2.5). W2-50 (2026-11-08) attributed the stalls and the tail to the display being off and did not reproduce the episode (9.4). Both §2.5 rows await an answer, so W2-50's Done is not met and W2-47's re-gate waits for them. **Answered 2026-11-09 (user decisions R26 and R27):** the episode closes as environmental (user activity during an evidence run), and gate 3 in the shipped default configuration can be claimed once the W2-47 re-gate passes. The `driver_stall` rule is unchanged; `measure_performance.ps1` holds the display on and treats a run that overlaps a Kernel-Power 506/507 display-off event as invalid evidence, and the sample-932 tail is closed as display-off. W2-50's Done is met once that harness change lands; it landed on 2026-11-09 (`feat(horde): measure_performance holds the display on and rejects display-off runs [W2-50]`), so W2-50 is done.
- **W2-52 before W2-49.** The `level_grant_paid` shim moves three Personal GOLDEN state digests, so it lands before the wave-2 re-bless (R16).
- **The R18 items in W2-47:** the sustained 15-20 FPS VSync-on stress episode, the 1050x700@1.35 VSync-on p99 interval of 19.1-19.7 ms, the script-level battlefield primitive and vertex gates in `measure_performance.ps1`, and any egui_probe/present_wait span-ordering item not fixed in wave 1, including the `baseline.v3` re-measure on the 7abb0ab spans (§2.5; R29, 2026-11-09: re-measured in W2-47 before any interface_total re-gate, gates only tighten). R30 (2026-11-09): every perf pass first waits for a quiet machine and rejects a run with a CPU load sample of 90% or more. R31 (2026-11-09): the interface_total re-gate decision is an interleaved A/B against the wave-1-close executable (ab03da9), B's median at most 1.05 × A's per size, not the absolute baseline. **Done (W2-47, 2026-11-10; VALIDATION.md "W2-47 re-gate on the wave-2 stress fixture", `PERF_BUDGET.json acceptance.w2_47`):** baseline.v3 re-measured (9.4; the gated pair kept its W1-37 values under R22); every gate pass exits 0 on the new stress fixture (dev and playtest, VSync on and off), but the dev -VsyncOff pass only on a rerun of a first invocation that had a valid progressed-peak small over (12.7565 ms against 10.0, masked to exit 2 by invalid runs), so that gate is not claimed clean pending the §2.5 row "A valid over in a pass masked by invalid runs" (corrected in the W2-47 review). Answered by R34 (2026-11-10): the over stands, G1.9 dev -VsyncOff progressed-peak is not claimed clean at W2-47, and W5-11 re-gates it. The A/B passes at B / A 0.962 / 0.963 after its build fix; gate 3 in the shipped default configuration is claimed (R26); the small-size VSync-on p99 interval was measured as hour-dependent and in the swap-chain acquire, not attributed further, and gates nothing.
- **Pending human checks (§11, R23).** The four wave-1 checks (camera feel, stepped damage pop, whole-pixel text placement, sheet upscale at 2.5x) and the integrated-GPU run stay pending.

**Wave-2 close (R25 review condition, R36, 2026-11-13).** The wave-2 review is closed at HEAD a9fc30a. The close review ran from 869b15e to a9fc30a (187 commits, all `[W2 review]`), after the W2-49 re-bless (e3f52fa); wave 2 as a whole is 295 commits since the wave-1 close (af530f0). **Spans (W1 review, 2026-11-07).** The W1-61 values in brackets, and baseline.v3, were measured on the probe spans from before review commit `7abb0ab`. That commit narrowed egui_pass to `EndPass` itself and moved surface configuration out of present_wait. So interface_total (same or lower) and cpu_frame (same or higher) from `7abb0ab` on are not like-for-like with them. The "W1 review at bee4236" and "867f786" values are the two re-runs at HEAD on the new spans (9.4, "Goal-1 acceptance re-run at HEAD" and "attempt 2"); the dev executable was byte-identical in both, the commits between them being docs only. The peak row stays at 867f786: attempt 2 ran only the three gate passes. baseline.v3 itself was not re-measured then. See 9.4 and the open item in 2.5. (W2-47, R29: baseline.v3 is now re-measured on the 7abb0ab spans, 9.4.)

Median earned at runs 3 / 5 / 7 / 9 / 11 / 13 over tier 1-6 cost: 1.37 / 0.87 / 1.07 / 1.56 / 1.88 / 2.23. R28 (2026-11-09) accepts the W2-08 move and re-baselines: later commits diff the default `pacing 60 --runs` output against `target/production-after/w2-41/pacing.txt` (sha256 e19f06694be215fe05068c542cb3f56e8741d2ed959aa5db03ba9d68d6bbe65c), not `w1-69/pacing.txt`. W2-22 (progression settlement, 2026-11-09) moved neither. The shipped catalogue still authors no level grant and no milestone, so settlement pays 0 coins and grants no item; it only claims the reached frozen ranks, as the removed wave-1 shim did. Before is the last recorded output of each: pacing `w2-09/pacing.txt` (W2-14, W2-16 and W2-17 moved no balance and did not re-run it) and smoke `w2-16/smoke.txt`. After, at W2-22: `--smoke` (`target/production-after/w2-22/smoke.txt`, PASS, sha256 c47b083a...eb8969ae) is byte-identical to `w2-16/smoke.txt`, and `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs --out target/production-after/w2-22/pacing.txt` (sha256 0510b062...f2574879) is byte-identical to `w2-09/pacing.txt`. The 20-seed acceptance was not re-run: the default and 20-seed sweeps run the same rules, and no reward is authored. The smoke and pacing move that ruling E2 expects comes with the authored values, which the W2-22 review handed to W2-54 (§2.5).

W2-42 (the pacing profiles that need other remits' commands, 2026-11-09) moved neither. It adds the registry rows `manual-sell`, `auto-sell`, `takeover`, `farm`, `easy`, `hard` and `long` and the plan variants `PacingSell::{ManualExtras, Auto}`, `PacingDefender::RecruitWhenReady` and `PacingFarm::ReplayAfterDefeats(u32)`; the three legacy profiles keep the solo plan and their code path. `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs --out target/production-after/w2-42/pacing.txt` (sha256 0510b062...f2574879) is byte-identical to `w2-22/pacing.txt`, and no rule moved, so smoke was not re-run. The new rows at one seed (`--profile expert-deu --profile manual-sell --profile auto-sell --profile takeover --profile farm --profile easy --profile hard --profile long`, `target/production-after/w2-42/profiles.txt`, sha256 15e7f5b9...5db09ac6; not committed), runs to first victory / first liberation / tree exhausted: expert-deu 2 / 15 / 27, manual-sell 3 / 13 / 25, auto-sell 5 / 17 / 29, takeover (Luxembourg) 2 / 5 / 43 with the first takeover at run 7, farm (novice) 6 / 19 / 30, easy 2 / 10 / 26, hard 5 / 18 / 30, long as expert-deu (first takeover > 150). Selling drops moves the expert's first victory because `recommended_node` reads owned items (as W2-08 found). Manual-sale coins reach the wallet but not `cumulative_coins`, whose definition (design-progression-tree §11) counts only auto-sold coins. The takeover plan hands every stage a recruit lost back to the survivor, otherwise one starter-loadout recruit loses the same stage for the rest of the profile (measured before that rule: second liberation > 60 runs). The takeover and farm columns do not isolate their plan: `takeover` also starts in Luxembourg and `farm` also plays the novice aim, so each differs from expert-deu in two fields (R51). `equip-drops` (G5.6) is not in the W2-42 row and stays unimplemented (`equip_drops` is still rejected). The W2 review implemented it later (147f422, `equip_drops_profile_wears_dropped_upgrades`; waves.md W2-42 note): the solo loop supports `PacingPlan.equip_drops`, only the strategy bot still rejects it, and the default output did not move.

W2 review (2026-11-10): the W2-42 rows above are one seed, and design-progression-tree §"Acceptance" runs `long` at 5 seeds (informational). All eight rows at 5 seeds, on the post-W2-49 rules (so after W2-54's level grant and milestones; the one-seed rows above predate them): `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- --profile expert-deu --profile manual-sell --profile auto-sell --profile takeover --profile farm --profile easy --profile hard --profile long --seeds 5 --out target/production-after/w2-review-b8/profiles-5seed.txt` (sha256 6d8b3fc1...95dcdfa8, not committed; about 5 minutes with the release build). Cells are the lower median [p25, p75], every seed reaching each milestone; runs to first victory / first liberation / second liberation / tree exhausted: expert-deu 4 [3, 4] / 15 [14, 15] / 18 [17, 18] / 27 [26, 27]; manual-sell 3 [3, 4] / 13 [13, 14] / 16 [16, 17] / 25 [25, 26]; auto-sell 4 [3, 4] / 15 [14, 16] / 18 [17, 19] / 27 [26, 28]; takeover (Luxembourg) 3 [2, 4] / 5 [5, 6] / 11 [10, 12] / 41 [39, 43], first takeover 7 [7, 8]; farm (novice) 3 [2, 3] / 16 [16, 17] / 19 [19, 20] / 27 [27, 28]; easy 2 [2, 2] / 10 [10, 11] / 13 [13, 14] / 24 [24, 24]; hard 5 [4, 5] / 15 [14, 15] / 18 [17, 18] / 27 [26, 27]; long equals expert-deu on every milestone, with no takeover in 150 runs in any seed. The rows are informational: none is a wave-2 acceptance, so `tests/pacing_targets.rs` does not sweep them. The one gate on them, G4.13 (`auto_sell_profile_within_one_run_of_default_at_tiers_1_to_3`, release), is wave-4 work (W4-l) and joins `pacing_targets` there.

W2-43 strategy pacing before tuning (the strategy rows on the harness, 2026-11-09) moved neither: the three legacy profiles keep the solo loop, and `pacing 60 --runs` (`target/production-after/w2-43/pacing.txt`) is byte-identical to `w2-42/pacing.txt` (sha256 0510b062...f2574879). The goal-3 table: `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- --strategy --seeds 20 --out target/production-before/strategy-pacing.txt` (sha256 85b7ace5...0b474ad6, not committed; about 8 minutes on 22 threads). S1 (`strategy`, novice, gear first, Luxembourg, 120 runs): liberations 2 / 4 / 10 at runs 8 [8] / 18 [18] / 42 [45] and 34 / 84 / 206 minutes of battle and camp; 3,154 [3,212] coins per minute; 602 battle and 60 camp minutes; the Operations layer unlocks at run 18; 19 [23] outbreaks and no overrun, so 0 reopens over the 10 liberations after the unlock in every seed (G3.12 passes). S2a / S2b (Germany's 3-stage neighbours, stop at the fourth liberation): 9 [9] / 9 [9] runs to free 3 neighbours after Germany, a ratio of 1.00 against the 0.67 target (G3.11 fails); 684 / 773 coins per minute. Expeditions settle (S1 a median 367, S2b 18) and bring coins (16,185 and 1,367) but clear no stage in any seed: the bot sends recruits on their starter loadout, as the design specifies, and they lose every stage (the W2-42 takeover row saw the same). W4-c owns making G3.11 pass, through balance with evidence (design-campaign-defenders §7 risk 7), not through the harness.

W2-54 (the authored level grant and milestones, 2026-11-09) moved both, on purpose: the latest default output is `target/production-after/w2-54/pacing.txt` (sha256 902cd895...c7f7d738), and smoke's Personal stage 1 now takes three attempts. The rows, the smoke lines and the W2-54 20-seed acceptance are in §9.6, "W2-54 moved the default pacing output and smoke".

W2-03 goal-5 "before" (2026-11-07, post-W1-69 rules): `cargo test --locked --release -p horde_core --test pacing_targets -- --ignored --nocapture`, output in `target/production-before/pacing_targets_goal5_before.txt` (not committed; the sweep took about 62 s). Every seed keeps Fortifications <= Mara <= Operations, but the Mara and Operations medians are equal. Both failures are wave-4 work (W4-p).

W2-04 goal-6 "before" (2026-11-07, post-W1-69 rules): the same command, now 11 tests (`--test-threads 1` for a stable print order), output in `target/production-before/pacing_targets_before.txt` (sha256 fa2f5b86...720be0, not committed; both sweeps took about 59 s). These starts are not clamped yet, because the clamp lands with the difficulty bands (ruling W-10). The four failures are wave-4 work (W4-04..n), apart from coverage, which W2-05 unblocks.

### 9.6 Enemies overlap (W1-69): pacing and smoke

User decision of 2026-11-06 (§2.2). Pacing: `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs`, on the parent `63168c1` (before) and on W1-69 (after), 60 runs per profile, as runs / battle minutes.

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU (before → after) | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |
|---|---|---|---|
| First victory | 4 / 13 → 3 / 9 | 3 / 9 → 5 / 17 | 2 / 6 → 3 / 10 |
| First liberation | 14 / 60 → 14 / 58 | 14 / 59 → 17 / 69 | 5 / 18 → 6 / 22 |
| Tier 2 affordable | 5 / 17 → 5 / 17 | 5 / 17 → 6 / 20 | 5 / 18 → 5 / 18 |
| Tier 3 affordable | 7 / 25 → 7 / 25 | 6 / 21 → 8 / 28 | 6 / 21 → 6 / 22 |
| Tier 4 affordable | 7 / 25 → 7 / 25 | 7 / 25 → 9 / 32 | 7 / 26 → 7 / 25 |
| Parallel defenses unlocked | 17 / 72 → 17 / 71 | 17 / 72 → 20 / 82 | 8 / 30 → 9 / 34 |
| Defeats in 60 runs (runs) | 5 (1, 2, 3, 8, 10) → 5 (1, 2, 4, 8, 10) | 5 (1, 2, 4, 7, 8) → 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12) | 2 (1, 3) → 3 (1, 2, 5) |
| Coins over 60 runs | 269,900 → 269,927 | 269,546 → 248,312 | 306,599 → 299,150 |

Runs are harder, as expected: every enemy at a wall or the line now attacks, instead of one per lane with the rest queued behind it (59 enemies in contact against 19 at tick 420 of the progressed-peak fixture). The novice profile takes the shift: 3 more defeats, 2 of them after run 10, and its first victory moves from run 3 to run 5. The expert profile is unchanged within noise. Nothing is retuned in W1-69. This is the input for wave 2's pacing work (W2-01 baseline, W2-49 re-bless).

Wave 2's baseline is the post-W1-69 output, not wave 0's. The W1-69 run is saved byte-for-byte (UTF-8, no BOM, LF) as `target/production-after/w1-69/pacing.txt`, and its smoke stdout as `target/production-after/w1-69/smoke.txt` (not committed, like every `target/` file). Wherever a wave-2 design or pin says the default pacing output "reproduces pacing.txt" or smoke "equals smoke.txt" (W2-01's pin in `waves.md`, the "Integration rulings" of the remit designs, design-campaign-defenders §8), it means these two files (for pacing until R28, 2026-11-09: from W2-08 on, the latest recorded default output, `target/production-after/w2-41/pacing.txt`). `target/production-before/pacing.txt` and `smoke.txt` stay the wave-0 record of §1.2 and §1.3.

Smoke (`cargo run --locked -p horde-defense -- --smoke`): PASS before and after. Before is wave 0 §1.3; gameplay did not move between wave 0 and W1-68 (GOLDEN and TRACE 0/6), and it was not re-run on `63168c1`. Failed attempts: Personal 2 → 1 (stage 1 victory at attempt 2 instead of 3), Npc 1 → 3 (stage 1 victory at attempt 3 instead of 2, plus one stage-2 defeat). The legacy autonomous run is still a Defeat: 33 kills, 110 coins in 119.0 s before; 49 kills, 163 coins in 136.0 s after. Personal stage 5 is a Victory in 253.8 s with 284 kills and 2,653 coins (254.2 s, 284 kills, 2,741 before). Npc stage 5 is a Victory in 256.0 s with 150 kills and 1,564 coins (250.0 s, 146 kills, 1,615 before).

**W2-08 moved the default pacing output (W2-08 review, 2026-11-08; ruling pending, §2.5; accepted by R28, 2026-11-09, which re-baselines on `w2-41/pacing.txt`).** design-loot-economy ("Digest moves") expected smoke and pacing to stay byte-identical across the loot series, and §5.2 chose `loot_only` over "All purchasable" for that reason. The premise was wrong for pacing. The default profiles never equip or sell drops, but `recommended_node` reads owned items ("Train for your owned ..."). W2-08's larger drop pool (18 instead of 12 tier-1 definitions) changes which item drops, so a different node is learned; expert run 2, for example, learns `smg_training` instead of `second_wind`. Smoke did not move. `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs --out target/production-after/w2-08/pacing.txt` at W2-08 (re-run on the review fixes, which change no pick) is saved as `target/production-after/w2-08/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 547f7b4e...cb2e4d3c; not committed). It no longer equals `target/production-after/w1-69/pacing.txt` (sha256 5bf2aec2...4f3fa0), so the W2-01 pin wording (waves.md) does not hold after W2-08. Runs / battle minutes, w1-69 → w2-08:

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |
|---|---|---|---|
| First victory | 3 / 9 → 5 / 17 | 5 / 17 → 6 / 20 | 3 / 10 (unchanged) |
| First liberation | 14 / 58 → 16 / 65 | 17 / 69 (unchanged) | 6 / 22 (unchanged) |
| Tier 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 affordable | 3 / 5 / 7 / 7 → 3 / 6 / 8 / 9 | 3 / 6 / 8 / 9 → 3 / 6 / 9 / 10 | 3 / 5 / 6 / 7 (unchanged) |
| Fortifications / Mara / Recruitment unlocked | 3 / 14 / 14 → 5 / 16 / 16 | 5 / 17 / 17 → 6 / 17 / 17 | unchanged |
| Parallel defenses / Country operations unlocked | 17 / 23 → 19 / 25 | 20 / 26 (runs unchanged; operations at 108 → 107 battle min) | unchanged |
| Defeats in 60 runs (runs) | 5 (1, 2, 4, 8, 10) → 7 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12) → 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 13, 14) | 3 (1, 2, 5) (unchanged) |

The W2-08 commit reports that the 20-seed release acceptance (`pacing_targets --ignored`) keeps the W2-01 medians: first victory 3 [2, 5], tiers 3 / 5 / 7, Liberation(2) 17 [16, 18], tree exhausted 25 [24, 26]. Only the one-seed default output moved.

**W2-26 moved the default pacing output (W2-26 review, 2026-11-08).** The cause is balance on purpose: the in-country front ramp (design-enemy-balance §3, ruling W-5). A country stage at depth `stage/(count - 1)` multiplies enemy health and arrival by `1 + 0.18 x front_ramp_pct/100 x depth` (Normal 1.18 on a final stage), so later stages field more and tougher enemies and pay more coins. The boss damage rank term also starts at rank 5. Smoke did not move (`target/production-after/w2-26/smoke.txt` equals `smoke_before.txt`). `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` on the parent `f8812aa` reproduces `target/production-after/w2-08/pacing.txt` byte for byte (saved again as `w2-26/pacing_before.txt`). At W2-26 the output is saved as `target/production-after/w2-26/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 2849040d...59b68bcb; not committed). It is the latest default output and replaces `w2-08/pacing.txt` for the next wave-2 diff. Runs / battle minutes, w2-08 → w2-26:

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |
|---|---|---|---|
| First victory | 5 / 17 (unchanged) | 6 / 20 (unchanged) | 3 / 10 (unchanged) |
| First liberation | 16 / 65 → 17 / 70 | 17 / 69 → 17 / 67 | 6 / 22 → 6 / 21 |
| Tier 2 / 3 affordable | unchanged | unchanged | 5 / 18 → 5 / 17, 6 / 22 → 6 / 21 |
| Tier 4 first node | unchanged | unchanged | 5 / 18 → 8 / 29 |
| Mara / Recruitment unlocked | 16 / 65 → 17 / 70 | 17 / 69 → 17 / 67 | Mara 6 / 22 → 6 / 21, Recruitment 7 / 25 → 6 / 21 |
| Parallel defenses unlocked | 19 / 78 → 20 / 83 | 20 / 82 → 20 / 80 | 9 / 34 → 9 / 33 |
| Country operations unlocked | 25 / 103 → 26 / 108 | 26 / 107 → 26 / 105 | 19 / 76 (unchanged) |
| Defeats in 60 runs (runs) | 7 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11) → 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 13, 14) → 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 11) | 3 (1, 2, 5) → 3 (1, 2, 4) |
| Coins over 60 runs | 253,389 → 271,241 | 247,914 → 268,254 | 299,150 → 325,826 |

The country-stage render fixtures move with it. `peak_render_fixture` and `maximum_progression_render_fixture` deploy stage 4 of their active country on Normal, so their encounter now carries a front ramp of `1 + 0.18 x 4/(count - 1)` (about 1.031 on peak's 24-stage USA front) on enemy health and arrival. The practice scenes built on them (peak, progressed-peak, max-lanes) and the stress and save-clone fixtures (`deploy_country_stage(stage, ..)`) move the same way. Captures and perf re-gates compared against pre-W2-26 baselines include this move; `render_fixtures_carry_their_stage_front_ramp` (tests/two_axis_threat.rs) pins it. GOLDEN deploys without a country and does not move.

- `goal6_lux_hard_at_most_1_5x_normal` FAIL → PASS: Normal 5 [5, 6] → 6 [6, 7], Hard 8 [8, 8] → 9 [8, 11]. Hard is now exactly at the 1.5x limit (9 ≤ 9). This check passed at W2-04 (Normal 6, Hard 8) and already failed on the parent: a commit after W2-04 and up to `f8812aa` moved LUX Normal from 6 to 5, and that move is not recorded. W2-26 makes it pass again, with no margin.

W4 tuning keeps `front_ramp_max` within [0.17, 0.25] (ruling W-5). Raising it widens the Hard/Normal gap, and the Hard limit now has no margin.

**W2-32 moved the default pacing output (2026-11-08).** The cause is balance on purpose: past rank 4 enemy health and damage grow on the late slopes (`growth_knee_rank` 4, `health_per_rank_late` 0.04, `damage_per_rank_late` 0.018), `pressure_floors` rows 5..=28 raise the floors and carry the composition from tier 4 to tier 11 (ranks 10 / 20 / 40 field tiers 7 / 10 / 11), and bosses gain armor with rank (Personal ×`1 + 0.02 × (min(r, 50) - 4)`, NPC `+ (r - 4) / 8`). Ranks 0-4 are unchanged (`ranks_zero_to_four_snapshot_pinned`), so GOLDEN, GAMEPLAY_TRACE, V1-V6 and smoke do not move (`target/production-after/w2-32/smoke.txt` equals `w2-26/smoke.txt`). `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` on the parent `51bd4e3` (exported tree, own target directory) reproduces `w2-26/pacing.txt` byte for byte (saved again as `w2-32/pacing_before.txt`). At W2-32 the output is `target/production-after/w2-32/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 45e5b794...77f3c6da; not committed), now the latest default output. Every run before rank 5 is identical; later wins take longer and pay more:

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |
|---|---|---|---|
| First victory, tiers 1-4, Fortifications | unchanged | unchanged | unchanged |
| First liberation, Mara, Recruitment | 17 / 70 → 17 / 71 | 17 / 67 → 17 / 68 | 6 / 21 (unchanged) |
| Parallel defenses unlocked | 20 / 83 → 20 / 84 | 20 / 80 → 20 / 81 | 9 / 33 (unchanged) |
| Country operations unlocked | 26 / 108 → 26 / 109 | 26 / 105 → 26 / 108 | 19 / 76 → 19 / 78 |
| Defeats in 60 runs (runs) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12) (unchanged) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 11) (unchanged) | 3 (1, 2, 4) (unchanged) |
| Coins over 60 runs | 271,241 → 346,158 | 268,254 → 353,989 | 325,826 → 442,890 |

Captures and perf re-gates compared against pre-W2-32 baselines include this move.

**W2-33 moved the default pacing output and smoke (2026-11-08).** The cause is balance on purpose: elites (design-enemy-balance §5). From rank 3 a director wave spawn is an elite with chance `elite_bp(r)` (200 bp at rank 3, +65 a rank, 1500 from rank 23; Easy ×0.5, Hard ×1.5), rolled by a keyed hash that draws no combat RNG. An elite has health ×1.5 (NPC hit points too) and pays reward ×4 and xp ×3, plus one affix (hardened, frenzied, vicious, shielded or volatile). Ranks 0-2 are unchanged, so GOLDEN, GAMEPLAY_TRACE (both rank 0) and V1-V6 do not move. The measured elite fraction at rank 13 is 8.54% (1,730 of 20,250 eligible director spawns, `elite_fraction_at_rank_13_is_8_5_pm_0_5_over_20k`). The elite forced drop is not in this commit (W2-35), so the extra coins come only from the reward ×4. `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` at W2-33 is `target/production-after/w2-33/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 774449b7...af118054; not committed); the parent's output is `w2-32/pacing.txt` (W2-32 is the parent). Every run before rank 3 is identical; later runs diverge as elites change the fights:

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |
|---|---|---|---|
| First victory, tiers 1-3, Fortifications | unchanged | unchanged | unchanged |
| Tier 4 first node | unchanged | unchanged | 8 / 29 → 7 / 25 |
| First liberation, Mara, Recruitment | 17 / 71 → 15 / 60 | 17 / 68 → 20 / 82 | 6 / 21 (unchanged) |
| Parallel defenses unlocked | 20 / 84 → 18 / 74 | 20 / 81 → 23 / 95 | 9 / 33 (unchanged) |
| Country operations unlocked | 26 / 109 → 24 / 100 | 26 / 108 → 29 / 121 | 19 / 78 → 19 / 77 |
| Defeats in 60 runs (runs) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12) → 6 (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7) | 8 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 11) → 11 (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14) | 3 (1, 2, 4) (unchanged) |
| Coins over 60 runs | 346,158 → 500,174 | 353,989 → 439,013 | 442,890 → 617,281 |
| Battle time over 60 runs | 15,735 s → 15,706 s | 16,051 s → 15,959 s | 16,286 s → 16,389 s |

The expert profile's two stage-5 defeats (runs 11-12) became victories, so it liberates Germany two runs earlier; the novice profile stalls on stage 4 (rank 3, elites at 2%) for five defeats instead of two, so it liberates three runs later. The one-seed defaults swing both ways; the 20-seed medians below barely move.

Smoke (`cargo run --locked -p horde-defense -- --smoke`, PASS) moved only at stages 4-5 (ranks 3-4), as design-enemy-balance §14 predicts; every attempt count is unchanged. `target/production-after/w2-33/smoke.txt` against `w2-32/smoke.txt`: Personal stage 4 Victory 220.5 s, 225 kills, 1,972 → 1,995 coins; Personal stage 5 Victory 253.8 → 253.4 s, 284 → 282 kills, 2,653 → 2,941 coins; Npc stage 4 Victory 223.0 → 220.0 s, 118 → 122 kills, 1,223 → 1,345 coins; Npc stage 5 Victory 264.0 → 259.0 s, 138 → 163 kills, 1,502 → 1,728 coins.

The render fixtures stage their initial hostiles through `spawn_enemy`, which never rolls elite, so their first frame does not move. Their live directors do roll: peak (rank 4, 265 bp) and the rank-50 fixtures (1500 bp) can field elites once they tick, so motion captures and perf re-gates compared against pre-W2-33 baselines include this move.

**W2-35 moved neither pacing nor smoke (2026-11-08).** The elite forced drop (ruling E8) adds one item per elite kill on the `drop_tier` Some path, picked by the keyed ELITE_PICK hash when only the elite term fires, so the combat RNG draws exactly what it drew before and the fights do not change. The pacing profiles never equip recovered loot (`equip_drops` is false), so `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` at W2-35 (`target/production-after/w2-35/pacing.txt`, not committed) is byte-identical to `w2-33/pacing.txt` (sha256 774449b7...af118054), and `--smoke` (`w2-35/smoke.txt`) equals `w2-33/smoke.txt`. W2-34 touched only horde_game, so W2-33's evidence is the parent's. The stash growth from forced elite drops (design-loot-economy "Risks" item 3) is measured in wave 4.

**W2-36 moved the default pacing output (2026-11-08).** The cause is balance on purpose: boss phases (design-enemy-balance §6). Past rank 4 every boss may enter one phase transition (Hard two, a Capstone one more), at 50% and then 25% of its hit points: it holds for 90 ticks (no movement, no attack countdown), then fights on with its kind's profile (Breacher speed ×1.4 and armor ×0.6, Caller call interval ×0.6, Siege Brute recovery ×0.6 and speed ×1.2). Ranks 0-4 off a Capstone are unchanged, so GOLDEN, GAMEPLAY_TRACE (rank 0, no country), V1-V6 and smoke do not move: `--smoke` at W2-36 (`target/production-after/w2-36/smoke.txt`, PASS) is byte-identical to `w2-35/smoke.txt`. `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` at W2-36 is `target/production-after/w2-36/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 f8000150...017da5f2; not committed); the parent's output is `w2-35/pacing.txt` (= `w2-33/pacing.txt`). **W2-39 moved the default pacing output and smoke (2026-11-08).** The cause is content on purpose: the country difficulty bands and the start clamp (design-enemy-balance §8, ruling W-10). `scripts/generate_world.py` now sets each country's difficulty to `(lo + fnv1a64("difficulty:" + id) % (hi - lo + 1)) / 100` in the band of its stage count (≤ 3 stages 85-115, 4-9 100-135, ≥ 10 120-160): the 242 countries span 0.85-1.60 with 50 distinct values and mean 1.049, and 235 of them changed. A deployment's enemy health and reward multiply by the difficulty and enemy speed by its square root, and the campaign's start country now plays it clamped to [0.95, 1.05] (`Country::effective_difficulty`). So Germany, the expert and novice start, plays 1.04 instead of 1.06, and Luxembourg, the gear-first start, plays the clamped 0.95 (raw 0.92) instead of 1.03; Austria, the first front the expert opens, is 0.85 (was 1.03). GOLDEN, GAMEPLAY_TRACE (no country) and V1-V6 do not move, and `PROVISIONAL` stays None. Smoke's seeded campaign lines are unchanged, but its legacy autonomous run (a Germany start) moves from "Defeat: 49 kills, 163 coins in 136.0s" to "Defeat: 70 kills, 240 coins in 174.0s" (`target/production-after/w2-39/smoke.txt`, PASS, sha256 c47b083a...eb8969ae, against `w2-36/smoke.txt`). The v6 equipped-sale recording's replay pin moves from fa528d88f6639802 to 711acf17d72357c2 (VALIDATION.md, "Strict sale and ruleset 2"). `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs` at W2-39 is `target/production-after/w2-39/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 f8856265...7918d5a0; not committed); the parent's output is `w2-36/pacing.txt` (W2-37 moved no pacing). Every profile diverges from run 1:

| Milestone | expert, tree first, DEU | novice, tree first, DEU | novice, gear first, LUX |

**W2-41 moved the default pacing output (W2-41 review, 2026-11-08).** The cause is content on purpose: the eight decision-19 nodes (ids 170-177, design-progression-tree §5). The W2-41 commit body said pacing need not be re-run because "the new nodes only add milestone-gated purchases, which tier_cost excludes"; that is wrong. `tier_cost` (`sim/pacing.rs`) sums every node at depth <= d with no gate filter; the exclusion of `minimum_victories >= 5` nodes is design-progression-tree §4's wave-4 tier definition (W4-p) and has not landed. The new nodes sit at depths 2 (`pistol_double_tap`, `smg_spray_control`), 3 (`shotgun_pump_drill`, `shield_breaker`, `blast_discipline`, `rifle_breath_control`) and 4 (`marksman_steady_aim`, `mg_belt_feeder`), so tier 1 stays 930 coins, tier 2 goes from 2,790 to 5,190, tier 3 from 4,975 to 11,675 and tier 4 from 6,655 to 15,655, and the tree is exhausted only with 177 nodes. `learn_recommended` also buys the new nodes once a run has 5 committed victories, which changes the fights after that. GOLDEN, GAMEPLAY_TRACE and V1-V6 do not move, and `PROVISIONAL` stays None. Smoke does not move: `--smoke` at W2-41 (`target/production-after/w2-41/smoke.txt`, PASS) is byte-identical to `w2-39/smoke.txt`. `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs --out target/production-after/w2-41/pacing.txt` at W2-41 is saved as `target/production-after/w2-41/pacing.txt` (UTF-8, no BOM, LF; sha256 e19f0669...d6bbe65c; not committed); the parent's output is `w2-39/pacing.txt` (W2-40 added only tests). The first diverging run is the first that buys a decision-19 node: expert run 12 (`shotgun_pump_drill`, `marksman_steady_aim`, `shield_breaker`), novice DEU run 13 and novice LUX run 13.

**W2-09 moved the default pacing output (2026-11-09).** The cause is the weighted single-draw DropTable on purpose (plan LE-5, design-loot-economy §3): a `drop_tier` Some pick now weighs each pool entry by its drop-tier step (6 / 3 / 1 / 1) and a trained Ranged class x3, with the same one combat-RNG draw. The default profiles never equip or sell drops, but `recommended_node` reads owned items, so where a different item drops a node is learned one run earlier or later. GAMEPLAY_TRACE, V1-V6 and smoke do not move: `--smoke` at W2-09 (`target/production-after/w2-09/smoke.txt`, PASS) is byte-identical to `w2-41/smoke.txt`. **W4-01 and W4-03 before/after (closing phase, 2026-11-15; ISO-7).** Neither row wrote its tables when it landed (both commit bodies leave them to the closing phase, R36), and the W4-p "before" below (1b59e20, on the wave-4 branch before integration) already holds W4-01 to W4-03, so this block isolates them. On the integrated branch W4-01 (75d73723) sits on f461c72d, not on its wave-4 branch parent 79b7c0a, and W4-02 (8107c2d8) sits between W4-01 and W4-03 (1b6fc341). Each commit's `horde-defense` tree was extracted with `git archive` into `target/production-after-wave4/src/<commit>/` and run there against the shared target dir, after `cargo clean -p` of the workspace members in both profiles (a tree extracted under the target root is otherwise taken as fresh against the previous tree's build, and the first attempt measured one binary four times). Commands: `cargo run --locked --release -p horde_core --example pacing -- 60 --runs --out .../pacing.txt`, `cargo run --locked -p horde-defense -- --smoke`, and at f461c72d, 75d73723 and 1b6fc341 the W4-p block's 20-seed `cargo test --locked --release -p horde_core --test pacing_targets -- goal --ignored --nocapture`. Outputs are in `target/production-after-wave4/commits/<commit>/` (`pacing.txt`, `smoke.txt`, `pacing_targets_goal.out.txt`; UTF-8, LF; not committed).
- 79b7c0a and f461c72d reproduce the wave-2 close byte for byte: `pacing.txt` sha256 902cd895...c7f7d738 and `smoke.txt` 846f0b4c...425fe50, the files in `target/production-after-wave2/`. The wave-3 and wave-5 picks and the wave-2 review fixes moved neither, so f461c72d is a valid "before" for W4-01.
- W4-02 moved neither: 8107c2d8 equals 75d73723 in both files (its lines are presentation reads).

W4-01 is balance on purpose (rulings W-6 and W-9): the expert's one new defeat is run 11 on DEU stage 6, the first Regional checkpoint (boss health x1.2), and the first liberation and every later unlock come one run later. Where no defeat is added, the Regional and Capstone reward multipliers (x1.2, x1.4) raise the coins: novice DEU +25,099, LUX +35,408. W4-03 is balance on purpose (design-progression-tree §1): steady_hands gives +4 damage instead of +8 and hardened +2 armor instead of +3, so the early runs are weaker. The expert loses run 2 at DEU stage 1 and its first victory moves to run 3. The novice buys the same opening nodes with a weaker aim and loses DEU stage 1 five times: it wins first at run 6 instead of 3, loses 10 runs instead of 6 and frees DEU 4 runs later. The gear-first LUX profile does not lean on these nodes and keeps every milestone.

Result: no listed lever is kept, and `progression.json` and `enemy_catalog.json` are unchanged. Inside the G6.17 band (TTK p90 at most 60 s) every variant leaves 32 or 33 windows out and a 46-50 win streak, the baseline's own numbers. Raising a lever only lifts the first windows past 25% (the early runs, where the survivor is weak) and leaves the late windows at 0%. `front_ramp_max` 0.25 is not shipped: `goal6_lux_hard_at_most_1_5x_normal` is green since W4-p at 0.18 (Hard 13 against Normal 9), and 0.25 turns no red row green, puts one more window out (33) and moves the Easy median onto its cap of 6. The front-ramp pins (`front_ramp_final_over_first_normal_is_at_least_1_35`, the 1.3924 Normal ratio) therefore stay as they are. No digest moves, so `PROVISIONAL` stays None.

Why the levers do not reach G6.11 and G6.12 (the `pacing` example, `60 --csv --profile expert-deu --seeds 20`, 100 runs per bucket):

| Runs | Shipped: defeats | Shipped: rank after (median) | Shipped: damage dealt to the survivor per run (median) | Late health 0.25: defeats | Late health 0.25: rank after (median) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1-5 | 67 | 0 | 71 | 67 | 0 |
| 6-10 | 58 | 3 | 48 | 58 | 3 |
| 11-15 | 19 | 6 | 0 | 35 | 5.5 |
| 16-20 | 7 | 11 | 0 | 47 | 8 |
| 21-25 | 0 | 16 | 0 | 8 | 12 |
| 26-30 | 0 | 21 | 0 | 1 | 17 |
| 31-35 | 0 | 26 | 0 | 0 | 22 |
| 36-40 | 0 | 31 | 0 | 14 | 27 |
| 41-45 | 0 | 36 | 0 | 82 | 29 |
| 46-50 | 0 | 41 | 0 | 98 | 29 |
| 51-55 | 0 | 46 | 0 | 100 | 29 |
| 56-60 | 0 | 51 | 0 | 98 | 29 |

- On the shipped data the expert survivor takes no archetype damage in the median run from run 11 (runs 11-15: level 28, rank 6) and loses no run after run 20. The rank still rises by one per victory, to 51 at run 60, where enemy health is 2.9x. Elites, affixes, the front ramp and the capstone change runs the survivor already wins without being hit.
- A steeper slope does not give a steady defeat share. It gives a wall. The survivor's power stops growing at about run 30 (level 50, tree exhausted at 28), the rank rises on a victory and never falls on a defeat, so the campaign climbs until the next rank cannot be beaten and then loses nearly every run there (late health 0.25: the median campaign stops at rank 29 and loses 98-100% of its runs from run 46; boss TTK p90 110 s). With the boss floors held flat past rank 4 the same slope moves the wall out of the 60 runs again (lowest window 0.0%, streak 38), so inside 60 runs the wall is mostly the boss fight, which G6.17 caps.
- The same holds outside the row's list, measured only to inform the decision and not shipped: `damage_per_rank_late` 0.05 / 0.10 / 0.20 (0.018) leaves 32 / 33 / 33 windows out and a 49 / 47 / 45 streak, and `arrival_per_rank` 0.02 / 0.04 (0.01) leaves 33 / 32 out with a 49 streak (TTK p90 99.7 s at 0.04).

G6.11 and G6.12 need a user decision (§2.5, "G6.11 and G6.12 stay red after the listed enemy levers"). Smoke before and after the loop is a closing-phase item (R36); with no data change the two runs are the same build.

Verdicts: G3.10 `strategy_profile_reports_liberations_2_4_10_and_coins_per_minute` PASS; G3.12 `fewer_than_five_outbreak_reopens_over_ten_liberations` PASS (median 0 [p90 0]); G3.11 `parallel_frees_three_stage_neighbours_within_two_thirds_of_solo_runs` FAIL ("parallel needs 1.00 of the solo runs, above 2/3"). The repricing moved S1 by one run at liberations 2 and 4 and the Operations unlock by one run; nothing else moved a verdict.

Why G3.11 fails (S2b, seed index 0, read from the run history; one seed, not a sweep). Germany falls at run 16 and the second liberation (Austria) at run 19, which unlocks Parallel. The roster is empty until the camp before run 21, when Survivor network (1,700 coins since W4-p) is owned and three recruits join. From then to the stop at run 25 the three recruits leave on 15 single-stage expeditions (Belgium, Czech Republic, Denmark, stage 1 each time) and lose all 15: they last 43-92 s with 3-10 kills and 13 health at threat rank 13-17, where the survivor's main runs last 260-310 s with 420-600 kills and 300-390 health. Two things follow:

- Timing alone already misses the target. A third neighbour free after run 22 is 6 runs, the most 2/3 of 9 allows. If every expedition from the camp before run 21 won at its first try, the first expedition-freed neighbour would be free after run 23, which is 7 runs (0.78).
- No expedition can win. The recruit fights under the Npc rules, and at these ranks that model does not hold a stage on any gear a campaign has.

Levers tried, each on S2b seed index 0 with scratch probes that are not committed (indicative; no 20-seed sweep, since none cleared the one-seed bar):

`strategy_config.parallel_unlock_freed` (design-campaign-defenders §7 risk 7) was not swept: with no stage cleared in any variant above, an earlier unlock changes nothing. `Game::defender_power` rated every recruit of the item-level rows 999 (Strong) at threat 14, so the rating does not predict these losses.

W4-c ships no data change and moves no digest: no value of the strategy configuration, the gear table or a preset makes a recruit win three stages in a row at its first try, which is what the target needs from the bot as designed (recruits on their starter loadout, design-campaign-defenders §1.13). G3.11 needs a user decision (§2.5, "G3.11 stays red"). The 20-seed re-run of the three goal-3 tests at HEAD is a closing-phase item (R36).

The novice survivor loses more early (the first windows rise past 25%) and still loses no run from about run 25: every solo variant reaches 0.0% windows and a 39-50 win streak. Gear first is stronger than tree first, not weaker.

G6.11 and G6.12 stay red on the measurement profile and need a user decision (§2.5). No digest moved: the registry gains a row and the acceptance reads it; no GOLDEN or GAMEPLAY_TRACE input changed.

W4-14 Npc combat balance (2026-11-14, R59; fbedee2 and 93321b8 on b771d2d). Systematic debugging first: an instrumented headless probe (a scratch integration test, not committed) plays stage 1 of the six 3-stage DEU neighbours (BEL, CHE, CZE, DNK, LUX, NLD) on three seeds, Normal preset, threat rank 0, Germany liberated, and records per run the outcome, the seconds, kills, spawns and the peak field, shots and hits, the Npc's maneuvers (aim, reload and idle turns), the attacks, hits and kills by range, the damage taken by archetype, and hit points, field size and contact count every second; the same stage and rank under the Personal rules (the smoke bot's geometric aim, starter pistol) runs beside it. Cells below are wins of 18.

| 17 lanes x 28 yd, rank 0, stage 1, starter loadouts (b771d2d) | Npc (Mara, Balanced) | Personal |
|---|---|---|
| Won | 0 | 0 |
| Seconds | 147-210 | 198-228 |
| Kills / spawned | 43-77 / 70-123 | 117-148 / 167-204 |
| Peak field | 27-46 | 50-69 |
| Shots / hits | 72-108 / 54-94 | 288-348 / all |
| Npc turns: aim / reload / idle | 49-71 / 10-16 / about 12 | - |
| Damage taken / maximum HP | 13-80 / 13 | 125-132 / 125 |

What the runs show: the Npc acts once per one-second turn and the field fills faster than it empties. It kills about 0.36 hostiles per second; the arrivals (the Personal schedule x `npc_arrival_factor` 0.9 x Normal's `npc_spawn_rate` 0.75) climb from 0.22 to 0.84 per second at stage 1's rate cap. By the boss (180 s) 30-40 hostiles stand on the field, 0-2 of them in contact; once they reach the line, 13 HP last 5-20 s. Checked and ruled out, each on the probe: gear and training reach the Npc (a recruit trained 60 points on level-15 gear wins 18 of 18 on Normal at rank 0); survival is not the lever (+20 extra HP: 0 of 18); expeditions place no structures by design (GDD section 18: the Npc builds nothing, a saved country layout is the player's); the Npc has no heal by design (Personal-only unlock); target choice is nearest-first with downed hostiles last, as GDD section 18 asks. The levers that move the outcome are skill (Guns +3 / +5 / +8: 3 / 17 / 18 of 18) and arrivals (spawn rate 0.5 / 0.4: 9 / 18 of 18).

Findings and fixes, one commit each:

1. Defect (fbedee2, `fix(horde): an Npc aims only when the aim raises its expected hits per turn`). The Npc called an aim useful below effective skill 14, and the Marksman aimed whatever the odds, two seconds whenever nothing stood within 5 yd. A one-second Aim costs a turn, so at effective skill 10-13 two unaimed shots land more expected hits (skill 12, Acc 5: 1.48 against 0.98). `aim_turns_that_pay` (pure) now picks the Aim turns that maximise expected hits per turn for every policy; attempts deployed before format 7 (`drop_tier` None) keep the old test, so V1-V6 do not move. A starter Marksman recruit spent 63-71 of about 180 turns aiming and won 2 of 18 where Mara (Balanced, same stats) won 18 at a 0.4 spawn rate; after the fix both win 18, and Mara on a 3 x 12 field goes 14 -> 18. At the shipped arrivals the fix alone wins nothing (0 of 18 both before and after). Fail-first: `marksman_shoots_unaimed_when_two_shots_land_more_than_one_aimed`, `npc_aims_a_long_shot_for_one_second_when_the_aim_pays` (tests/simulation.rs), `aim_pays_only_when_it_raises_expected_hits_per_turn` (sim/npc.rs).
2. Root cause (93321b8, `balance(horde): the Npc arrival factor follows the Npc action economy, 0.9 -> 0.5`). The Npc rules cap the kill rate (one action per turn: a perfect shot with the carbine fires 12 of 14 turns, 0.86 kills per second), and no gear or training lifts the cap, while the Npc arrivals sat at 0.675 x the Personal schedule, at the cap of a perfect shooter at stage 1 and twice what a starter kills. `npc_arrival_factor` falls to 0.5, the catalogue and saved-snapshot floor (Normal 0.375 x, Easy 0.35 x, Hard 0.45 x the Personal schedule); no enemy-curve value, Personal rule or preset moves. Fail-first: `npc_defenders_hold_stage_one_of_a_fresh_neighbour_at_low_ranks` (tests/npc_balance.rs, release-only: 18 stage-1 runs each for Mara on a main run at rank 0, a starter recruit and a recruit trained 20 points on real expeditions from a rank-15 campaign) read 0 / 0 / 7 of 18 on the parent and 17 / 17 / 18 after.

Evidence correction: W4-12's scratch probe (a recruit trained 60 points on level-15 gear won 0 of 18 at rank 0) ran on the legacy balance (`BalanceSettings::legacy`, `npc_spawn_rate` 1.0, `defender_health` 1.0); on Normal that recruit wins 18 of 18 at rank 0 even before W4-14.

Calibration (the probe after finding 1, wins of 18; `npc_arrival_factor` 0.9 -> 0.5; "trained N" spends N points Guns first and plays the rank the W4-12 rule gives it, at level-1 gear; 9 x 18 is a player of level 10, 17 x 28 one of level 25):

| Persona | 9 lanes x 18 yd | 17 lanes x 28 yd |
|---|---|---|
| Mara starter, stage 1, rank 0 | 0 -> 18 | 0 -> 17 |
| Marksman recruit starter, stage 1, rank 0 | 0 -> 18 | 0 -> 17 |
| the same, stage 2 (front ramp) | 0 -> 5 | 0 -> 5 |
| the same, stage 3 (Capstone) | 0 -> 0 | 0 -> 0 |
| trained 20 (rank 1) | 1 -> 18 | 9 -> 18 |
| trained 28 (rank 2) | 0 -> 16 | 0 -> 18 |
| trained 36 (rank 3) | 0 -> 7 | 0 -> 16 |
| trained 44 (rank 4) | 0 -> 2 | 0 -> 9 |
| trained 60 (rank 6) | 0 -> 0 | 0 -> 1 |
| starter at rank 5 / rank 10 | 0 / 0 -> 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 -> 0 / 0 |

Mara and a trained recruit hold stage 1 of a fresh country at low ranks; rank still decides (a starter loses from rank 5, training keeps a recruit level with its W4-12 rank to about rank 3), and the Capstone needs training and gear (a recruit trained 20 points wins it 11 of 18 in the 17 x 28 arena at factor 0.4, measured while choosing the value). A won stage pays more than a lost one (17 x 28 starter: median 299 coins, 237 before). Factor 0.4 held a little more (stage 2: 12-13 of 18; trained 44: 12-15) but puts an Npc snapshot's `arrival_multiplier` under the saved-snapshot floor of 0.5, which every Npc save then fails, so 0.5 ships.

Release acceptance, 20 seeds (`pacing --strategy --seeds 20`, `target/w4-14/strategy-before.txt` and `strategy-after.txt`, not committed; `pacing_targets` with the goal-3 tests and the goal5 and goal6 filters, 396 s), before (b771d2d) and after (93321b8):

|---|---|---|

| G3.10 S1 coins per minute | 3,339.0 [3,418.5] | 3,523.1 [3,601.7] |
| S1 expeditions settled / stages cleared / coins | 340 [353] / 0 [0] / 49,057 | 333 [345] / 12 [15] / 83,817 |
| S2b expeditions settled / stages cleared / coins | 15 [18] / 0 [0] / 2,120 | 15 [18] / 4 [5] / 4,100 |
| S2a / S2b coins per minute | 778.9 / 868.5 | 778.9 / 942.2 |
| G3.11 S2b / S2a runs to free 3 neighbours | 9 [9] / 9 [9] = 1.00, FAIL | 9 [9] / 9 [9] = 1.00, FAIL |
| G3.12 reopens over 10 liberations | 0 [0], PASS | 0 [0], PASS |
| goal5_* (first victory; tiers 1 / 2 / 3; Mara unlocked) | PASS | PASS: 3 [3, 4]; 4 / 8 / 12; run 16 [15, 17] |
| G6.11 / G6.12 on goal6-novice (R60: accepted red) | 32 of 37 windows out / 44 [50] streak | the same (the solo sweeps play no Npc) |

Why G3.11 stays red. The expeditions now clear stages (all of them stage 1s and 2s), but no starter recruit takes a Capstone, so no expedition liberates a 3-stage neighbour, and the bot never trains or regears its recruits (design-campaign-defenders §1.13). With the Npc arrivals at their floor (`npc_arrival_factor` 0.5 x `npc_spawn_rate` 0.25 = 0.125 x the Personal schedule, a scratch run) S2b's expeditions clear 7 [8] of 9 [12] stages, the Capstones included, and G3.11 reads 7 / 9 = 0.78: W4-c's timing finding (Survivor network, 1,700 coins since W4-p, is owned from the camp before run 21, so even winning every expedition at its first try the third neighbour falls 7 runs after Germany, and 2/3 of 9 allows 6). No Npc combat value makes G3.11 pass; W4-14 changes neither the acceptance bot nor progression prices (R59), so the row needs a new user decision (§2.5, "G3.11 stays red after the Npc combat balance pass").

`Game::defender_power` does not predict these outcomes (noted, not changed). Its Npc model reads the hit chance at the raw skill, with no range penalty, no Aim turn and no backlog of arrivals, and rates every defender against the starter at threat 0. Guns training therefore barely moves it (a recruit trained 20 points on Guns reads 98 at rank 1, below the starter's 100 at rank 0, and wins 18 of 18 where the starter wins 17; on the parent it won 7 and the starter 0, at the same ratings), and the recruits W4-c measured read 999 (Strong) while losing every stage. A band that says whether a defender holds a stage would need the range-penalised hit chance and the arrival rate against the turn-limited kill rate.

Digests (cause G, `PROVISIONAL` and `PROGRESSED_PROVISIONAL` with one cause row per commit, re-blessed by the closing phase): the three Npc `GOLDEN` and `GAMEPLAY_TRACE` runs (the Personal three unchanged), the two recruit-takeover `PROGRESSED` runs (whose window now starts 3000 ticks before the boss, `PROGRESSED_TAKEOVER_BOSS_LEAD`, since half the arrivals left seed 1's window without an elite; the Hard Capstone pins unchanged), and the v7 continuation (a new `V7_CONTINUATION_PROVISIONAL`, moved by finding 1: the fixture's root defense is Mara's). Re-recorded with their reasons: `MODE_OWNER_DIGEST`, the Npc reach-0 trace (sim/spitter_tests.rs) and `RANKS_ZERO_TO_FOUR_FNV`; `docs/PERF_BUDGET.json` `evidence.egui_measured` re-measured (the review fixtures fight in Npc mode; no gate moves). V1-V6, `V6_DROP_TRACE_FNV`, the `save_v7.json` pins and the frozen-catalogue pin do not move. Smoke (`campaign_smoke`): Personal unchanged; Npc stage 1 needed three attempts (b771d2d), still three after finding 1, one after finding 2, and stages 2-5 are each won at the first attempt.

W4-15 earlier recruitment (2026-11-14, R61; 63283a9 and 62f90d3 on 6764d2a). Two levers, one commit each, measured in release over 20 seeds (`pacing --strategy --seeds 20`, `target/w4-15/strategy-a.txt` and `strategy-b.txt`; all 16 `pacing_targets` tests, `pacing-targets-a.txt` and `pacing-targets-b.txt`, 586 s and 590 s; none committed). Before is HEAD 6764d2a, whose code is W4-14's 93321b8 (`target/w4-14/strategy-after.txt` and `pacing-targets-after.txt`). W4-16 (R62, the Npc `defender_power` readout) has not landed: R61 measures G3.11 after it, so the closing phase re-runs these rows once W4-16 changes the bot's `RecruitAs` choice.

Why the two levers. The strategy bot recruits in camp before it learns, so Survivor network bought in the camp after the second liberation (the camp that opens Parallel command) puts the recruits on the roster a camp after the first expedition could leave; at 1,700 coins S2b learns it in that camp or later in 12 of 20 seeds. And a starter recruit loses every Capstone, so no expedition liberates a neighbour (W4-14).

1. Price (63283a9, `balance(horde): Survivor network 1,700 -> 1,500 coins ...`). Only `recruitment_network`'s price changes (no id, gate or effect); tier 3 costs 19,630 and the tree 96,720, and the six tree rules stay green. The tree-first bot buys the cheapest affordable node that acts now, so the lower the price, the fewer structure ranks Survivor network waits behind; below 1,500 it falls into Meet Mara's camp. Sweep (`LASTLIGHT_SKILLS`, the same release binaries): Mara / Operations medians 16 / 19 at 1,700, 16 / 18 at 1,500, 16 / 17 at 1,450, 16 / 16 (`goal5_unlock_order_fortifications_mara_operations` red) at 1,400, 1,300 and 1,200. At 1,500 S2b owns the node by the camp of the second liberation in 18 of 20 seeds (seed indices 5 and 18 stay a camp late). Fail-first `survivor_network_opens_a_camp_earlier_than_the_w4_p_price` (tests/strategy_pacing.rs): "Survivor network owned after 61 runs, the second liberation after 19". G3.11 does not move on this lever (9 / 9).
2. `train_companions` (62f90d3, `feat(horde): the train_companions plan option ...`). `PacingPlan::unsupported` and the strategy bot's `unsupported` no longer reject it; `neighbours-parallel` (S2b) sets it, S1 and S2a do not. In camp after recruiting and before the tree and gear spending, each Ready recruit buys training points (`BuyRecruitPoint`) up to 12 awarded, the level-1 allowance (`COMPANION_TRAINING_POINTS`, so buying never lifts the expedition rank off advancement level 1), wears slot by slot the free stash item of the highest Npc `Campaign::upgrade_gain` that keeps `Campaign::expedition_threat_rank` where it was (`EquipRecruit`), then trains Guns with every unspent point (`TrainRecruit`). Fail-first `train_companions_is_a_supported_strategy_plan_option` ("assertion failed: row(\"neighbours-parallel\").plan.train_companions") and `train_companions_trains_each_recruit_before_its_expedition_through_commands_only` ("recruit 1 leaves with 0 awarded points").

Choosing the step (scratch probes, not committed; S2b, release, 20 seeds, the 1,500-coin node; lower median runs to free 3 neighbours after Germany, solo 9):

| Variant | Expedition stages cleared per seed | Runs |
|---|---|---|
| No step | 3-5 | 9 |
| Train only, after the tree spending (the purse is empty: no point bought) | 5-8 | 9 |
| Train only, first, to 12 / 20 / 28 / 36 awarded | 6-8 / 6-9 / 4-6 / 2-5 | 9 / 7 / 9 / 9 |
| Train first to 12 and equip (shipped) | 9 of 9 in 18 seeds | 6 |
| Equip with no bought point, or train after the spending and equip | 8-9 | 6 |
| Train first to 12 and equip, Survivor network at 1,700 | 9 | 7 |

- Training alone does not take a Capstone: 20 bought points put a recruit at rank 1 (Guns +5), its first two clears lift the awarded points to 24 and the rank to 2, and most Capstones are then lost (stages 1 and 2 are won). More training raises the rank faster than the recruit's strength, and the bought points also take the coins Parallel command needs in the camp after the second liberation (at 28 it is bought a camp late).
- The equip step is what wins. The stash holds tier-4 drops of item level 1-4 from the early runs (a Longshot marksman rifle at level 3-4, Bastion or Reactive armour at level 1-2), and the W4-12 rule prices them at rank 0, because it reads item levels (floored over the four slots) and not tiers. Equipped, the recruits clear all 9 stages at their first try in 18 seeds. Noted, not changed: the rule's constants are W4-12's (R61 keeps them).
- The price lever is still needed: with the step and the node at 1,700 the recruits join a camp late in most seeds and the median is 7 (0.78).

G3.11 passes at the bound: 6 is the floor the timing allows, because Parallel opens after the second liberation (Austria, on the main run) and a single-stage expedition clears one stage per camp, so the first expedition neighbour falls three runs later, beside the main run's second neighbour. The goal-6 table is no worse on the gate profile. The takeover row's first takeover moves from run 20 to 19 at seed 0.

Digests: the price moves all four `PROGRESSED` runs (`PROGRESSED_PROVISIONAL`, cause "W4-15 price", class G: the gameplay traces move): their staging buys every node the gates allow in catalogue order from a purse that covers them all, so the node set, the gear and the in-window play do not change; the 200 coins it keeps stay in the campaign purse, which the core hash and the settled trace lines carry; all four still win. `train_companions` moves none (pacing-harness bot code). `GOLDEN`, `GAMEPLAY_TRACE`, the v7 continuation, V1-V6 and the save fixtures do not move. A pre-existing `clippy::manual_is_multiple_of` failure in tests/progression_tree.rs (from 39225d6) is fixed in c9c92d4.

W4-16 Npc power readout (2026-11-14, R62; 9f18943 on dcafb50). `Game::defender_power` kept its Personal model; its Npc model was rebuilt and calibrated against the W4-14 calibration table above (design-campaign-defenders §1.11, "As built (W4-16)"). The model plays the stage the deployment inputs describe as a deterministic fluid of hostiles, one one-second turn at a time: the stage level's arrival schedule with the snapshot's floors and arrival multiplier (`npc_arrival_factor` 0.5 folded in) times `spawn_rate` and `npc_spawn_rate` and the wave pool's members per pick; hostiles walk in single file from the field depth at the arrivals' spacing; the defender spends one action a turn on the nearest, at the range-penalised skill after the Aim turns that pay (`sim::npc::aim_turns_that_pay`) and the reload turns, against the pool's landed shots per kill (its dice through each hostile's armor and dodge); the backlog past what the approach holds stands at the line and hurts the defender; the boss walks in at the boss time and the stage is won when it falls. Gear and training reach it through the profile. The rating is the heaviest arrival load (times the stage's own) the defender still wins at, over a fresh Mara's in the default campaign (exactly 100, so the G2.3 pins hold; bands unchanged). Tests, all in the normal suite (`tests/npc_power.rs`): `npc_power_reads_the_range_penalised_hit_chance`, `npc_power_counts_only_the_aim_turns_that_pay`, `npc_power_weighs_arrivals_against_the_turn_limited_kill_rate`, `guns_training_raises_npc_power_at_the_recruits_expedition_rank` (fail-first: "trained 20 at rank 1 ... rating: 98 ..., starter at rank 0 ... rating: 100") and `npc_power_band_agrees_with_the_w4_14_win_table` (fail-first: ten cells that lose most read Even, among them the Capstone at 85).

| Persona (W4-14 calibration table) | 9 x 18: rating (band), wins of 18 | 17 x 28: rating (band), wins of 18 | Parent (both arenas) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mara starter, stage 1, rank 0 | 101 (Even), 18 | 99 (Even), 17 | 100 (Even) |
| Marksman recruit starter, stage 1, rank 0 | 101 (Even), 18 | 99 (Even), 17 | 100 (Even) |
| the same, stage 2 (front ramp) | 66 (Weak), 5 | 65 (Weak), 5 | 92 (Even) |
| the same, stage 3 (Capstone) | 1 (Weak), 0 | 22 (Weak), 0 | 85 (Even) |
| trained 20 (rank 1) | 127 (Strong), 18 | 133 (Strong), 18 | 98 (Even) |
| trained 28 (rank 2) | 96 (Even), 16 | 104 (Even), 18 | 96 (Even) |
| trained 36 (rank 3) | 81 (Weak), 7 | 89 (Even), 16 | 95 (Even) |
| trained 44 (rank 4) | 69 (Weak), 2 | 77 (Weak), 9 | 93 (Even) |
| trained 60 (rank 6) | 49 (Weak), 0 | 55 (Weak), 1 | 87 (Even) |
| starter at rank 5 / rank 10 | 39 / 18 (Weak), 0 / 0 | 38 / 21 (Weak), 0 / 0 | 90 (Even) / 76 (Weak) |

The test asserts that no persona winning 15 or more of 18 reads Weak, that every persona winning 5 or fewer reads Weak, and that a persona winning 8 or more stages more than another rates higher (the same persona across the two arenas included: trained 36 wins 16 at 17 x 28 and 7 at 9 x 18). The cells between (7 and 9 wins) are only ordered. "Parent" is the W2-21 model on the same inputs (stage cells with the deeper stage's snapshot): it rated every cell but rank 10 Even. Survival is not what the rating reads, as W4-14 measured (+20 HP: 0 of 18): an HT or HP gain moves `survival_s` but hardly the load held, so `outbreak_plan_recommends_the_strongest_selectable_defender` now makes its recruits stronger with Guns. Not modelled (recorded, not changed): the policies' melee and All-Out Defense, elites and boss phases, the Caller's summons, structures, the unlocked-boss archetypes (the pool is a fresh campaign's) and the HT rolls at 0 HP.

The strategy bot's choices that read the rating: only `RecruitAs` reads it in the acceptance bots (`best_archetype`, at the campaign's next threat; the takeover row's `SetNextDefender` picks the first ready recruit, and no bot reads `OutbreakPlan.recommended_defender`). At ranks 13-17 a Marksman starter now rates 9-15 and a Guardian 1-4 (both read Even or Weak under the parent), so the bot recruits differently in some offers: seed index 3 of the S1 row no longer resumes an interrupted country inside 40 runs (`s1_strategy_bot_acts_only_through_commands` now reads index 0; indices 0, 1, 5-7, 9 and 10 take every path at 40 runs). The Camp roster and the defender picker print core's rating and band (`roster_shows_core_power_and_status_labels` and the `ui::` suite pass unchanged); a fresh campaign reads as before (100, Even), and recruits at the campaign rank read Weak where they lose.

Smoke. `cargo run --locked -p horde-defense -- --smoke` (`smoke.txt`), PASS, exit 0. The `horde_core` smoke test (`cargo test --locked -p horde_core --test simulation campaign_gates_are_achievable_in_both_modes_without_rare_loot -- --nocapture`, seeds 1, 2026, 0x1A571167) passes, and the `campaign_smoke` example prints the same 12 attempt lines as `--smoke`. Against W4-03's `commits/1b6fc341/smoke.txt`:
- Personal (moved by W4-p, a9a842a2: the smoke camp buys the repriced recommended nodes; `target/w4-14/smoke-before.txt`, on the post-W4-p b771d2d, already has these lines): stage 1 attempt 2 209.5 s, 150 kills, 500 coins → 210.2 s, 149 kills, 498 coins; attempt 3 199.4 s, 151 kills, 622 coins → 211.2 s, 150 kills, 621 coins; stage 2 191.1 s, 168 kills, 979 coins → 207.6 s, 174 kills, 986 coins; stages 3-5 and attempt 1 unchanged. Failed attempts stay 2 (stage 1 x2).
- Npc (moved by W4-14: fbedee29 and 93321b8a, as `target/w4-14/smoke-aim.txt` and `smoke-arrival.txt` record): 8 attempts with 3 defeats → every stage won at the first attempt (stage 1 197.0 s, 51 kills, 289 coins; stage 5 253.0 s, 98 kills, 1,199 coins).
- The legacy autonomous Npc run: Defeat, 70 kills, 240 coins in 174.0 s → Victory, 50 kills, 290 coins in 189.0 s ("The sector boss was defeated"). It is an Npc run. The W4-14 files record only the campaign lines, so the commit that moved it (W4-p's Npc trap-blueprint price read in `smoke_prepare`, or W4-14's Npc rules) was not isolated.
- W4-04..n changed no data, so one run at HEAD is its before and after (the W4-04..n block above). W4-03's own before and after are in the "W4-01 and W4-03 before/after" block.
- Audio: `--smoke` returns before an `App` exists (`main.rs`, the `LaunchAction::Smoke` branch), so it plays no sound. The carried audio checks (SFX through `CueRequest`, stolen `PannedClip` voices ending through `PlaybackSettings::DESPAWN` with no `Fading` pile-up) need a windowed run with audio and cannot be confirmed by `--smoke`. They stay with the listening pass (§11).
- Build note: the first `--smoke` attempt ran a stale `target/debug/horde-defense.exe` linked from the git-archive tree `target/closing-tests2/src` (9101dbe3). That tree shares the workspace-relative unit hash, so cargo took it as fresh against this tree. The binary was rebuilt after removing its fingerprint, and the result is the same, because `horde_core` is identical at 9101dbe3 and HEAD. The game binary was built with another agent's uncommitted `horde_game` edits in the tree. `--smoke` reads only `horde_core`, whose tree is clean.
- Build note, cause and fix (closing, release-packaging 1): cargo keeps a source under the target dir relative to the target dir, so a tree extracted under the shared CARGO_TARGET_DIR and built there writes its own paths into this workspace's fingerprints (the same unit hashes), and touching this tree's files no longer rebuilds them. Removing the bin fingerprint was not enough: after that rebuild the dev `horde_core` and `horde_audio` units still named `target/closing-ui2/src` (built 2026-11-15 19:19; 93 of the bin's 245 dep-info sources). Their sources are byte-identical to HEAD's, as are `target/closing-ui3`'s, so no result moved, but a `horde_core` edit would not have reached a dev build or test link. No release or playtest unit was taken over, so the pacing evidence is unaffected and no pacing row needs a re-run (the fix changes scripts only). `measure_performance.ps1` (review, shipped), `capture_review.ps1`, `capture_motion.ps1` and `package_playtest.ps1` now build through `Invoke-HordeBuild` (`scripts/lib/Native.ps1`): it reads the built exe's dep-info (`Get-ForeignBuildSource`) and, when a source lies outside the workspace or under the target dir (OUT_DIR files excepted), cleans `horde-defense`, `horde_core` and `horde_audio` in that profile and builds once more; a build still foreign throws (exit 3). It repaired the dev build here (93 foreign sources to 0, log `target/closing-release-packaging/repair/`), and the rebuilt binary's `--smoke` stdout equals `smoke.out.txt` byte for byte (`target/closing-release-packaging/smoke.out.txt`). Evidence re-check, by the asset root in the stderr of every run from 2026-11-16 02:45 to 05:50 (local time): `fix-tests2` and `final` ran `target/closing-tests2` on purpose (9101dbe3, as 10.6 states); `w7-11` and `w7-11-aborted` ran `target/w711` (d53cfe0f, built with its own target dir, whose dep-info names only that tree); the only workspace-exe run is the 05:42 smoke. No closing evidence ran the stale binary unknowingly; the four `fix-ui*-stale-exe` folders of 2026-11-15 each ran the previous extracted tree (asset roots `closing-ui2`, `-ui3`, `-ui4`, `-ui5b`) and were re-run at the time from the right tree (`fix-ui3` to `fix-ui6`). AGENTS.md now says to build an extracted tree with its own CARGO_TARGET_DIR.

Measurements the wave-4 rows left to this phase, from `pacing --profile expert-deu --seeds 20 --csv` and `--profile farm --seeds 20 --csv` (`pacing/*.csv`, `pacing/extras.txt`). Boss rank is computed from each run's stage and the country's stage count (`FrontPosition::boss_rank`):
- Boss TTK by boss rank (`expert-deu`, the G6.17 sweep): Sector 26.9 s [p25 23.3, p90 45.6] over 669 kills, Regional 46.9 s [46.1, 71.8] over 20 (DEU stage 6, once per seed), Capstone 39.8 s [36.4, 54.6] over 358; pooled 31.4 s, as G6.17. Both new ranks' medians sit inside 20-60 s, and the Regional p90 is above 60 s. On `farm` (novice aim): Regional 68.3 s, Capstone 55.0 s, pooled 38.4 s, reported only.
- Capstone replay against the frontier (design-enemy-balance "Numbers to record", at most 1.1×): `farm` replays the stage before the frontier, which is never a Capstone. Both sweeps also replay liberated countries' Capstones once the map offers no new stage, so each such replay's coins per minute is read against the median of the same seed's frontier victories within two runs. `expert-deu`: median 1.031× [p25 0.986, p75 1.077, max 1.273] over 85 replays (2,839 against 2,733 coins per minute), 16 above 1.1×. `farm`: 1.041× [0.980, 1.099, max 1.251] over 80, 19 above 1.1×. The median passes. No acceptance row gates this check, so it is reported.
- Results leads with Upgrade after a first defeat (W3-28): run 1 is a defeat in all 20 `expert-deu` seeds, with 332 [min 283, max 357] coins earned (battle, level grant and milestones). The node learned first in the next camp is the recommended node, `steady_hands` (120 coins) in 13 seeds and `smg_training` (100) in 7. Every seed affords it with at least 176 coins to spare, and none learns nothing (`farm`: 297 [268, 346], at least 148 to spare).

R58 save clone (`save_clone` example, `ITEM_COUNTS` 1,000 / 2,500 / 5,000 / 20,000; dev and playtest builds of HEAD; reports in `target/production-after/closing/save-clone/`). Another workspace's release `cargo test` ran on the machine throughout. The first set (`save-clone/*.json`) sampled the load only before and after each run (2-76%). The second set (`save-clone/low-load/`) started each run after a 60 s window with a mean load under 35% and sampled the load every 2 s during the run (run means 7-60%, max 87%; no sample at 90%, R30). `save_snapshot` p99 in ms, three runs each, median in bold:

| Profile | Items | First set | Low-load set | Gate (1 ms) |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| dev | 1,000 | 0.465, 0.456, 0.492 | 0.455, 0.619, 0.552 (**0.552**) | pass |
| dev | 5,000 | 0.400, 0.484, 0.449 | 0.508, 0.498, 0.450 (**0.498**) | pass |
| dev | 20,000 | 0.718, 0.714, 0.727 | 0.813, 0.729, 0.825 (**0.813**) | pass |
| playtest | 1,000 | 0.610, 0.468, 0.549 | 0.430, 0.581, 0.419 (**0.430**) | pass |
| playtest | 5,000 | 0.794, **1.124**, 0.422 | 0.435, 0.747, 0.420 (**0.435**) | pass in the low-load set |
| playtest | 20,000 | **1.078**, **1.225**, 0.629 | 0.690, 0.875, 0.720 (**0.720**) | pass in the low-load set |

The gated fixture (1,000 items, dev and playtest) passes in every run. In the low-load set, 5,000 and 20,000 items pass in every run (worst 0.875 ms, playtest, 20,000). In the first set, three playtest runs went over 1 ms. Run 2 (5,000 at 1.124, 20,000 at 1.225) read 11% load before and 75% after. Run 1 (20,000 at 1.078) read 44% before, while the foreign build was compiling. Those runs are recorded, not discarded. The cost under load sits at the 1 ms line, as VALIDATION.md "R58" found after the `Arc<str>` remedy (11778f2). R58 changes something only if the gate fails: the quiet closing perf run (`measure_performance.ps1 -SaveClone`, which carries the 20,000-item report) decides, and its lever is VALIDATION.md's shared copy-on-write stash, which needs its own measurement.

Quiet closing perf run (2026-11-16, HEAD c34c39cd, `horde_core` unchanged since c9e6b0af): `measure_performance.ps1 -Scene peak -NoBuild -SaveClone`, once per profile, outputs in `target/production-after/closing/save-clone/quiet/{dev,playtest}/` (`save-clone.json` sha256 59a8cf8b...1841faef dev, 7fa3879f...0a8751e5 playtest). The script rebuilt the `save_clone` example at HEAD and ran it only after its R30 quiet window (dev: mean 11.9% over 60 s, max 25%; playtest: 13.2%, max 22%; no foreign build process). The window took 31 and 29 minutes to come, because another workspace's `cargo test` cycles ran before it. Both exit 0, and every size passes. `save_snapshot` p99 in ms at 1,000 / 2,500 / 5,000 / 20,000 items: dev 0.464 / 0.433 / 0.395 / 0.683, playtest 0.428 / 0.354 / 0.416 / 0.760 (20,000-item max 0.936 dev, 0.912 playtest). The gate does not fail on valid evidence, so R58 changes nothing: the first-set overs were measured beside a foreign build, and the copy-on-write stash is not pulled. The scene runs in these directories are incidental (the playtest exe predates HEAD) and are not frame evidence.

The stage-1 render reads 5 LU under the model's -42: the model's scripted battle was busier than the seed-1 level-0 battle, and the model is good to about 3 dB (design-audio section 14). `dense_battle_60s_true_peak_at_most_0_95` passes today (true peak 0.080, short-term max -37.0 against -14) and is ignored only for its length.

A6-17 "after" (2026-11-14): the same command with `--include-ignored`, the shipped library (exporter v2, mastered) under the wave-6 policy at the default sliders 0.8 / 0.8 / 0.8 (Master / Combat effects / Atmosphere). The gate tests are green and no longer ignored; no runtime master limiter was added (R3).

Before the calibration, with the wave-6 assets and policy (A6-16 tip): stage-1 -32.3, rain -29.0, dense -27.3 LUFS at true peak 0.805. What the calibration set, and why:
- `MIX_CALIBRATION_DB` -3.1 to -2.0. The dense battle bounds the voice buses by true peak: its peaks are three or four transients that start on one frame (rifle, turret, impact, damage), 0.805 before the calibration. +1.1 dB leaves 0.875 against 0.95; one voice at the maximum sliders stays under 0.75.
- `AMBIENCE_CALIBRATION_DB` 0 to +11.2 and the default Atmosphere slider 0.5 to 0.8 (+15.3 dB together). The level-0 battle is one pistol (voices alone read -31.2 LUFS, and at most +1.4 dB of voice gain fits under the dense peak), and no music track is shipped (FU-AUD-MUSIC), so the bed carries the integrated loudness of the reference mix. The loops at the maximum sliders stay under 0.57. The adapter (`atmosphere_audio::update_ambience`) now multiplies the calibration in; before, the knob existed only in the offline render.
- Rain loop weight 0.38 to 0.10 (`SceneAmbience::from_theme`, `render::RAIN_WEIGHTS`). At 0.38 the rain bed read 9.3 LU over the dry bed, which no single bed level fits into a 4 LU window; at 0.10 it reads 1.3 LU over.

Closing re-measure (2026-11-15, after b0256a17): the same command with `--include-ignored --nocapture`, same sliders, re-run at the closing-phase tip that carries b0256a17. b0256a17 gave the reference scene's Results part the loot reveal (150 ms) and one coin batch (450 ms) on the `ResultsAudioGuard` timing (design-audio section 3: "stinger, LootReveal, CoinBatch"), and has `battle_frames` hear drops through the runner's `LootTracker`. The seed-1 battle drops nothing in 44 s, so only the Results seconds changed. These are the numbers `AUDIO_SURVIVAL.md` ships. The gate tests are green, and there is still no runtime master limiter (R3: TP 0.885 against 0.95).
